Mr. President, the high-profile lawmakers have made government policies a permanent loss of credibility for educators. This draft law has sparked great public debate. The changes planned to be made have taken the educators and students to the streets. The government must listen to these voices. This bill must be re-examined by the opinions of trade unions, NGOs, and educators. The bill must be prepared in a way that will eliminate the problems of education in our country before the government becomes a community reckoning. Unfortunately, that has not been done in this unilateral arrangement. The bill demands that teachers' job security be eliminated. After this arrangement, the government has made it difficult to become a teacher and has signed arguments. From now on, winning only KPSS will not be enough to become a teacher. Those who win KPSS will be subjected to performance within a year without disciplinary action. In addition, teachers who are successful in these matters will be tested in writing and verbally during the same year. The draft for oral examinations has never been specified on what basis. Those who succeed in all these evaluations will continue as teachers, and those who fail will not be able to teach. In other words, the government will appoint whomever it wants as a teacher. This arrangement worries about appointing 350,000 teachers waiting to be appointed. A draft of a law like this came up while we were waiting for a teacher to appoint. Unfortunately, the quality of education in Turkey is expected to rise to its level, while teachers' appointments and administrators are expected to take a different position. With this bill, the government can also terminate the duties of all senior executives, except for the Undersecretary of the National Ministry of Education. The bureaucrats appointed by previous ministers will be dismissed or removed from office by the current Minister. The duties of educators serving as school principals and vice principals in our country are also concluded by this bill. From now on, school principals and vice principals will be appointed for four years with the approval of the governor, with a proposal from the governor of the county education director without a test. Hundreds of managers, such as other arrangements, will cause the vice principal to become a victim. I hereby salute the High Council with my regards to the acceptance of our motion.
